########## [5] wild-lights
---- VERDICT: ASK-OWNER
---- FINDER SPEC:
⚠ DO NOT author a spellNotes row here. Print modifies _dancing lights_; 'dancing-lights' does not ship (the remaster folded it into Light), and our .d has already retargeted the text to Light — but the retarget makes the clause FALSE on two counts against our own shipped Light record:
(1) COUNT — the feat says "instead of up to four floating lights", which is Dancing Lights' four-orb effect. Remaster Light creates ONE orb; its only "four" is a cap on how many separate Light SPELLS you may have active at once, not four orbs from one casting. So "instead of up to four floating lights" describes nothing Light does.
(2) DURATION — the feat says "modify its duration to be 1 minute". Light's duration is "until your next daily preparations", so the modification is a strict, pointless downgrade rather than the upgrade it was over Dancing Lights.
CORROBORATION that this is not a data-entry slip on our side: Wanderer's Guide does not carry a Wild Lights record at all (`node scripts/wg-show.mjs wild-lights` → "No ability_block row whose name slugs to 'wild-lights'"), while it carries every other feat in this batch as an injectText. WG is a differ only, but its silence here matches the analysis.
Put to the owner: (a) rewrite the clause against remaster Light (drop the four-lights phrase, and either drop or invert the duration change) and then author {feats/wild-lights/spellNotes → spellId:'light'}; or (b) mark the feat legacy-blocked and leave it undelivered. Either way the current state — a .d that silently retargets to Light while no carrier delivers anything, and an AST that still says "dancing lights" — is the worst of both.
Same reader chain would apply once resolved: build.ts:5478-5490 → explain.ts:688 → SpellsTab.tsx:2226 → :241. Also note wild-lights is in the 2nd-level dedication pick list (featPickGrants.ts:164), so it is reachable early.

No carrier exists in any lane (0 wild-lights rows in effect-backfill/toggle-modes/consumable-modes/src rules; only featPickGrants.ts:164). WG silence reproduced. Choosing between a rewrite and a legacy-block is an editorial retarget call, not ours — escalate.

########## [6] IMPORTER-aon-facets-bodyOf
---- VERDICT: CONFIRMED
---- FINDER SPEC:
ROOT CAUSE (confirmed by execution, not inference). scripts/lib/aon-facets.mjs:308-312 `bodyOf(rec)` strips only the FIRST `<title>` element and returns the ENTIRE rest of the AoN page — on a multi-variant page that is every sibling variant's text. `activationOf()` (aon-facets.mjs:415-418) and `frequencyOf()` (aon-facets.mjs:448-469) then take the FIRST regex match anywhere in that page-wide body. Both are emitted unconditionally in the items block at aon-facets.mjs:913-917 (`const act = activationOf(rec); if (act) facets.activationCost = act;` / `const freq = frequencyOf(rec); if (freq) facets.frequency = freq;`), consumed by scripts/import-core-v2.mjs:284 `overlayContent()` which spreads facets over every overlaid record.

REPRODUCTION (I ran it): loading C:/trying ai 2/hh-data-export/without-images/data/equipment.json and calling the exported frequencyOf/activationOf returns, for ALL 25 docs equipment-407 / -407-* : freq={max:1,per:'day'} act={type:'actions',value:1} — i.e. Pale Lavender Ellipsoid's line stamped onto every stone; and for ALL 15 docs equipment-3055 / -3055-* : freq={max:1,per:'hour'} — Sprouting's line stamped onto every stone. Also equipment-470-591 (Wayfinder of Rescue) act={type:'actions',value:1} leaked from a sibling wayfinder.

SCOPE (measured over equipment/weapon/armor/shield/relic exports): 10,001 item docs, 4,686 sit on a page with `<title level="2">` variant sections and resolve to their own section. Of those, 265 receive a `frequency` and 102 an `activationCost` whose first page match lies in a NON-BASE sibling section — an unambiguous cross-variant leak. (A looser count — value present but the record's own section has no line — is 1,331 / 698; the difference is the legitimate 'Greater X functions as X' inheritance from section 0.)

GUARD (apply-ready): the correct section-splitter ALREADY EXISTS in the same file — `heldSpellsOf` at aon-facets.mjs:499-538 builds `sections` from the `<title level="2">` marks and selects `norm(section.title) === norm(rec.name)`. Extract that into a shared `variantBodyOf(rec)` and route `activationOf`/`frequencyOf` through it:
  1. If the page has no level-2 marks -> current `bodyOf` behaviour (unchanged).
  2. If it has marks and `norm(rec.name)` matches a section -> scan ONLY that section; if that section has no `**Activate**` / `**Frequency**`, fall back to SECTION 0 (the base entry) only, then return null. Never a sibling.
  3. If it has marks and the name matches nothing -> scan section 0 only.
Step 2's section-0 fallback is required and must not be dropped: Aeon Stone (Lavender and Green Ellipsoid) genuinely inherits Pale Lavender's Reaction/1-per-day via 'This functions as a pale lavender ellipsoid aeon stone', and Greater/Major grade variants inherit their base the same way.

REGRESSION TEST (assert against regenerated core.json): items['aeon-stone-consumed'].frequency === undefined; items['aeon-stone-sprouting'].frequency deep-equals {max:1,per:'hour'}; items['aeon-stone-flickering'].frequency deep-equals {max:1,per:'day'}; items['aeon-stone-western-star'].activationCost deep-equals {type:'actions',value:2}; items['aeon-stone-pink-rhomboid'] has neither field.

UNTIL THE GUARD LANDS, every per-stone row below is required, because applyBackfill (scripts/lib/apply-backfill.mjs:56-66) runs after the facet merge and is the only thing that can undo the synthesis.

Re-derived independently. aon-facets.mjs:308-312 bodyOf strips only the first <title>; activationOf:415-418 and frequencyOf:448-469 take the first page-wide match; emitted unconditionally at 913-917; only callers are those two lines (bodyOf's other users 365/391/405 untouched). I executed the real exports: all 25 equipment-407* -> freq {1,day} + act {actions,1}; all 15 equipment-3055* -> freq {1,hour}, act null (the em-dash `**Activate—Name**` form doesn't match). Scope re-measured: total 10001, own-section 4686, freqLeak 265, freqLoose 1331 — exact. Two evidence details are wrong (act source section; actLeak count) but the root cause, guard and regression asserts all hold.

########## [7] parser-parsefrequencies
---- VERDICT: CONFIRMED
---- FINDER SPEC:
scripts/import-core.mjs:673 — the multiplier is matched then thrown away by a NON-capturing group: `(?:\d+\s+)?`. Change to a capturing group and shift the unit index:
  673: `const re = /frequency\s+(once|twice|…|\d+)(?:\s*times?)?\s+(?:per|each|every)\s+(?:(\d+)\s+)?(day|hour|minute|round|turn|week|month)/gi;`
  678: `const n = Number(m[2]); out.push({ max: FREQ_WORDS[w] ?? (Number(w) || 1), per: m[3].toLowerCase(), ...(n > 1 ? { every: n } : {}) });`
Then scripts/import-core.mjs:695-697 buildCounters must emit both the field and the label:
  696: `counters.push({ id: i === 0 ? 'freq' : `freq${i + 1}`, label: `per ${(f.every ?? 1) > 1 ? `${f.every} ${f.per}s` : f.per}`, max: f.max, per: f.per, ...(f.every ? { every: f.every } : {}), resetsOnRest: !['week','month'].includes(f.per) });`
SECOND SITE, same defect: scripts/import-core.mjs:564 `parseFrequency` (singular, feeds `Item.frequency` at :1688) also drops the multiplier via `(?:\d+\s+)?`. Leave it alone unless Item.frequency gains a reader — see the engine finding. Note it also only accepts `per` (not `each`/`every`), so "once every 10 minutes" never reaches it.
With the parser fixed, the 48 overlay rows below become belt-and-braces (the overlay is absolute assignment and survives `npm run data`, so keep them).
---- VERIFY REASON:
All three cited defects are verbatim correct: import-core.mjs:673 `(?:per|each|every)\s+(?:\d+\s+)?(day|hour|...)` (non-capturing multiplier), :678 `per: m[2].toLowerCase()` (m[2] IS the unit), :696 `label: `per ${f.per}``; the twin at :564 has the identical `(?:\d+\s+)?` and accepts only `per`, feeding Item.frequency at :1688. I replayed the :673 regex with the multiplier captured over core.json items x core-descriptions .d (markdown flattened as flatDesc does) and reproduced the tally exactly: 69 `10 minute` matches, 1 `30 minute`, 68 records, 4 dice-cadence. ---- CORRECTED FIX:
Apply as specced, but read the priority correctly: this is a LEGACY-PATH fix, not the carrier. import-core-v2.mjs (what `npm run data` runs) contains no frequency parser at all and copies `counters` from the frozen public/core.foundry-backup.json, so editing import-core.mjs changes nothing in public/core.json until someone regenerates that backup via `npm run data:foundry`. The 48 overlay rows are the SOLE delivery, not belt-and-braces — do not let this finding downgrade them.
When applying, keep the FULL alternation (the spec's '…' is shorthand, not literal):
  673: const re = /frequency\s+(once|twice|thrice|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|\d+)(?:\s*times?)?\s+(?:per|each|every)\s+(?:(\d+)\s+)?(day|hour|minute|round|turn|week|month)/gi;
  678: const n = Number(m[2]); out.push({ max: FREQ_WORDS[w] ?? (Number(w) || 1), per: m[3].toLowerCase(), ...(n > 1 ? { every: n } : {}) });
  696: counters.push({ id: i === 0 ? 'freq' : `freq${i+1}`, label: `per ${(f.every ?? 1) > 1 ? `${f.every} ${f.per}s` : f.per}`, max: f.max, per: f.per, ...(f.every ? { every: f.every } : {}), resetsOnRest: !['week','month'].includes(f.per) });

########## [8] held-spell-rank-check.mjs
---- VERDICT: CONFIRMED
---- FINDER SPEC:
WHAT IT READS (verified line by line): (1) exactly ONE storage carrier — `item.heldSpells` (held-spell-rank-check.mjs:35-36 `const held = item?.heldSpells; if (!held || typeof held !== 'object') continue;`), the rank->ids map; (2) exactly ONE text carrier — `descs.items[id].d` (:37), matched by ONE forward-only regex `RANKED` (:29) that requires the number to sit IMMEDIATELY BEFORE the spell name and the name to be followed by `[.,;)]` or ` as|and|from|with|at|on`.

WHAT IT MISSES:
(a) TEXT SHAPE — the reversed print form `You cast <Spell> at <N>th rank` / `as a <N>th-rank spell` / `heightened to <N>th level`. This is how the whole Treasure-Vault/Player-Core-2 OVERCHARGE WAND family is written, and it is where the 31 items below hide. Also blocked: a trailing noun (`4th-rank Dream Message spell.` -> slug `dream-message-spell`, silently dropped at :56), a space instead of a hyphen (`4th rank Wall of Wind`), `(` after the name (`3rd-rank Fear spell (DC 38)`), `Nth-level` instead of `Nth-rank`, and `A or B` lists (`2nd-rank Harm or Heal`).
(b) NAME/ID SHAPE — for the graded wand family the printed rank is carried by the VARIANT TITLE (`Wand of Teeming Ghosts (3rd-Rank Spell)`) while the body says only `of the indicated rank`. The check never reads `item.name`, so for these records there is no number in the prose to find at all.
(c) STORAGE SHAPES never opened: `item.spellSlot.rank` (144 items, live reader build.ts:6416-6417 `{ [item.spellSlot.rank]: [it.heldSpell] }` + ItemDetail.tsx:201 picker filter), `item.innateSpells[].rank` (65 items, live reader build.ts:6516 -> castRank build.ts:6586-6627 -> innateRep build.ts:6649), `item.resonant.innateSpells[].rank` (18 items, build.ts:6530), `item.spellNotes` (10 items), and the same `innateSpells` shape on feats (303) / heritages (13) / backgrounds (8).

WIDENING SPEC for scripts/held-spell-rank-check.mjs:
1. NAME LANE (new, catches all 31): if `/\((\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)[- ]Rank(?: Spell)?\)$/i` matches `item.name` (or the id tail `-(\d+)(st|nd|rd|th)-rank(-spell)?$`), that N is the printed rank for every NON-CANTRIP spell in `item.heldSpells`; report when no key equals N.
2. REVERSED PROSE LANE: per held spell, build a regex from `content.spells[sid].name` and match `<Name>( spell| cantrip)?[^.;]{0,60}?(?:at|as an?)\s+(\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)[- ](?:rank|level)` and `<Name>[^.;]{0,60}?heightened to (?:the )?(\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)[- ]?(?:rank|level)`.
3. FORWARD LANE FIX: allow `[- ]` between number and `rank`, accept `level` as a synonym, allow an optional trailing `spell|cantrip|ritual`, and widen the terminator set to `[.,;)(—–]` plus end-of-string.
4. CARRIER SWEEP: also walk `item.spellSlot.rank` (compare against the name lane — currently all 144 agree, so this lane starts green), `item.innateSpells[]` and `item.resonant.innateSpells[]` with effective rank = `g.rank ?? spells[sid].rank`, skipping grants that carry `heightenHalfLevel`/`heightenAt`/`heightenBySkill` (level-dependent, no single printed answer) and at-will cantrips (rank 0 by design, same reason the existing :59 cantrip guard exists).
5. DIRECTION RULE (memory rule, must be encoded, not just documented): for evidence drawn from DESCRIPTION PROSE, only `printed > stored` is evidence. Graded variants share ONE description body in this corpus, so a lower number in the text is the base variant's clause, not this record's. Concrete refutation: `greater-trident-of-the-azarketis` stores hydraulic-torrent at 6 and its `.d` contains "You cast a 5th-rank hydraulic torrent" (the Item-11 base clause) — the widened matcher flags it and it is CORRECT as stored, because the same `.d` also carries the greater's own clause. Evidence drawn from the record's OWN NAME is per-record and is safe in BOTH directions — that is the only lane allowed to report `stored > printed`, and it is what catches wand-of-pernicious-poison-1st-rank-spell.
6. Keep the existing `storedAt` Set-per-spell semantics (an item may legitimately hold one spell at several ranks) and the cantrip early-return.
---- VERIFY REASON:
Re-derived line-by-line. scripts/held-spell-rank-check.mjs: RANKED at :29 (forward-only, terminator [.,;)] or as|and|from|with|at|on), held read :35-36, .d read :37, unheld-slug drop :56, cantrip guard :59; item.name is never read. I ran it: "112 item spell(s) carry an explicit \"Nth-rank\" qualifier. / held-spell-rank: ok" — verbatim. Carrier counts re-counted: heldSpells 734, spellSlot.rank 144, innateSpells 65, resonant.innateSpells 18, spellNotes 10, heritages 13, backgrounds 8. Reader chain confirmed at build.ts:6416-6418/6443-6444/6456, 6516, 6530, 6586-6627, 6649; ItemDetail.tsx:201/596/602; heightening.ts:119. I ran the proposed name-lane regex: exactly 31 mismatches, exactly one stored-above-print (wand-of-pernicious-poison-1st-rank-spell). spellSlot lane: 0/144 mismatch, starts green. Direction-rule example verified verbatim.
---- CORRECTED FIX:
Widen scripts/held-spell-rank-check.mjs as specced (name lane, reversed-prose lane, forward-lane terminator/[- ]/level widening, carrier sweep, direction rule, keep storedAt Sets + cantrip guard). Four corrections to the spec text: (1) feats carrying innateSpells is 302, not 303 (heritages 13 / backgrounds 8 are right). (2) The evidence's SpellsTab.tsx:83 quote is verbatim but sits inside signatureEchoIds; the display chain that actually consumes the item repertoire is SpellsTab.tsx:404/411 (rank list + flattened ids) and :749. (3) The widened check will start RED at 76 (31 name + 45 prose) and three of the prose 45 are permanent false positives unless guarded — add explicit skips for (a) `at <N>th rank or higher` conditional triggers (dragon-eye, heldSpells {1:[breathe-fire]}, .d "When used to cast Breathe Fire at 3rd rank or higher"), (b) `<N>th-rank or <M>th-rank <Spell>` player-choice lists (the-mountain-man, .d "You cast 2nd-rank or 4th-rank Enlarge"), and (c) later-boon-stage clauses (elder-seed, .d "you can cast it at 4th rank"). ########## [9] F1-heuristic-and-narrowed-predicate
---- VERDICT: CONFIRMED
---- FINDER SPEC:
CARRIER: C:\trying ai 2\pf2e codex\scripts\import-core.mjs:162, inside uuidLabel() (declared :160, doc comment :155-159). READERS: import-core.mjs:262 (label-less refs the PACK_KEY map covers) and import-core.mjs:267 (all other label-less @UUID/@Compendium refs) — both inside cleanDescRich's .replace chain, so whatever uuidLabel returns is the literal prose the record ships with.

CURRENT (:162):
  if (!seg || (/^[A-Za-z0-9]{12,}$/.test(seg) && !/[ -]/.test(seg))) return '';

FOUNDRY'S ACTUAL ID SHAPE — measured, not assumed. Walked every JSON under .import-src/pf2e/packs/pf2e and collected every `_id` at any nesting depth: 16,624 ids, of which 0 are not exactly 16 characters; the union of all characters used is exactly `0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz` (the 62-char alphabet of Foundry's randomID). The codebase already encodes this correctly one screen away: import-core.mjs:1426 uses `/^[A-Za-z0-9]{16}$/` with the comment "skip raw 16-char Foundry ids — those aren't names". Line 162 is the outlier.

WHY `{16}` ALONE IS NOT ENOUGH. Two of the 31 blanked segments are exactly 16 characters and are real words: `Prestidigitation` (18 occurrences) and `Inextinguishable` (1). Tightening `{12,}`→`{16}` still eats every Prestidigitation reference. The predicate must also reject word-shaped segments.

NARROWED PREDICATE (drop-in for :162):
  /** A Foundry document id is exactly 16 chars from [A-Za-z0-9] (verified: 16,624 pack ids, all
   *  length 16, alphabet 0-9A-Za-z). A capitalised or all-lowercase single word can collide with
   *  that length — `Prestidigitation`, `Inextinguishable` — so length alone is not the test; an id
   *  drawn uniformly from 62 characters is word-shaped with probability (26/62)^16 ≈ 1.6e-6. */
  const FOUNDRY_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9]{16}$/;
  const WORD_LIKE  = /^(?:[A-Z][a-z]+|[a-z]+)$/;
  function uuidLabel(ref) {
    const seg = (ref.split('.').pop() || '').trim();
    if (!seg || (FOUNDRY_ID.test(seg) && !WORD_LIKE.test(seg))) return '';
    return /conditionitems/i.test(ref) ? seg.toLowerCase() : seg;
  }

BLAST RADIUS OF THE NARROWING: zero regressions available. Across the whole pack tree there is not one label-less @UUID/@Compendium whose last segment is an actual Foundry id — all 31 distinct segments the current predicate blanks are real words. The id branch is currently a 100%-false-positive rule; the narrowed form keeps it only as a guard.
---- VERIFY REASON:
I read import-core.mjs myself: :162 is `if (!seg || (/^[A-Za-z0-9]{12,}$/.test(seg) && !/[ -]/.test(seg))) return '';`, uuidLabel declared :160 with the doc comment :155-159, and its only callers are :262 and :267 inside cleanDescRich (:198). :1426 verbatim: `const fromUuid = seg && !/^[A-Za-z0-9]{16}$/.test(seg) ? slug(seg) : null;`. My own sweep of .import-src/pf2e/packs/pf2e: every `_id` is exactly 16 chars over 0-9A-Za-z (0 exceptions); Prestidigitation and Inextinguishable are both exactly 16 chars, so `{16}` alone still eats them. One supporting claim is false (see fix), but it does not change the fix or its safety.
---- CORRECTED FIX:
Apply the drop-in at :162 exactly as specced (FOUNDRY_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9]{16}$/, WORD_LIKE = /^(?:[A-Z][a-z]+|[a-z]+)$/, uuidLabel returns '' only when FOUNDRY_ID && !WORD_LIKE). Correct two details in the comment/rationale before landing: (1) the tree DOES contain label-less refs whose last segment is a real Foundry id — .import-src/pf2e/packs/pf2e/ancestries/kholo.json and ancestries/tripkee.json each hold @UUID[Compendium.pf2e.journals.JournalEntry.45SK8rdbbxvEHfMn.JournalEntryPage.xBV8oGvecw7abZxc|FhIJtKcINEe7fipX] inside system.description.value; both are killed by the journals filter at :251 before uuidLabel runs, and the narrowed predicate would still blank them, so 'zero regressions' holds but the wording must be 'nothing that REACHES uuidLabel is an id'. (2) My count is 18,144 `_id` values across 16,363 pack files, not 16,624 — the length/alphabet claim is what matters and it holds.

########## [9] F2-dead-subcondition
---- VERDICT: CONFIRMED
---- FINDER SPEC:
The `&& !/[ -]/.test(seg)` half of import-core.mjs:162 is unreachable. `/^[A-Za-z0-9]{12,}$/` is fully anchored and its character class excludes both space and hyphen, so any seg reaching the second test already contains neither. Dropping it changes no behaviour and removes the misleading suggestion that the rule tolerates multi-word names. The narrowed predicate in F1 already omits it.
---- VERIFY REASON:
`/^[A-Za-z0-9]{12,}$/` is anchored at both ends over a class containing neither space nor hyphen, so `!/[ -]/.test(seg)` can never be false once the first test passes. The one JS escape hatch — `$` also matching before a trailing newline — is closed at :161, where seg is `(ref.split('.').pop() || '').trim()`. Removing the clause is behaviour-preserving, and F1's narrowed predicate already omits it.
---- CORRECTED FIX:
No separate edit needed: landing F1's replacement of :162 deletes `&& !/[ -]/.test(seg)` as part of the same change. If F1 is deferred, the standalone edit is to reduce :162 to `if (!seg || /^[A-Za-z0-9]{12,}$/.test(seg)) return '';` — but prefer landing F1, which fixes the real bug rather than only tidying it.

########## [9] F3-damage-census
---- VERDICT: CONFIRMED
---- FINDER SPEC:
METHOD. Re-ran the importer's own ref pipeline (the effects-drop at :248, journals-drop at :251, labelled-ref keeps at :253/:258/:259, label-less uuidLabel at :261/:267, @Damage at :270, macro/roll/@actor passes) over every description string in .import-src/pf2e/packs/pf2e — system.description.value plus every system.items[*].description — with the offending ref marked. Located each resulting hole inside the shipped public/core-descriptions.json `.d` by whitespace/markdown-insensitive anchor matching with an index map back to the raw string, so every `find` below is a verbatim slice of the shipped text.

TOTALS.
- 273 label-less refs match the current predicate across the pack tree; 31 distinct segments; every one of them is a real word (no Foundry id is ever blanked by this rule today).

########## [9] F12-detector-shape
---- VERDICT: REFUTED
---- FINDER SPEC:
TWO ADDITIONS. The prose SHAPES catch some of this damage but structurally cannot catch the list/table forms, and a source-diff mode catches all of it exactly.

(A) SHAPES entries. Append to the exported SHAPES array (scan-damaged-descriptions.mjs:40). BLOCKER: `normalise` (:65-70) does `.replace(/\r?\n/g, ' ')`, so any shape anchored on list structure will never fire. Add a per-shape opt-out and honour it in scan():
  // in SHAPES entries:  { key, re, why, raw: true }
  // in scan() (:76-82), replace `const text = normalise(v.d);` with:
  const norm = normalise(v.d);
  for (const s of SHAPES) if (s.re.test(s.raw ? v.d : norm)) hits.get(s.key).push(category + '/' + id);

  { key: 'empty-list-item', raw: true,
    re: /^[ \t]*-[ \t]*(?:\*\*[^*\n]*\*\*[ \t]*)?$/m,
    why: 'a bullet with no content — the spell/feat name that was its whole text was deleted ("- **8th:**" was "- **8th:** Disappearance")' },
  { key: 'empty-table-cell', raw: true,
    re: /\|[ \t]*\|[ \t]*[^|\n]/,
    why: 'a table cell emptied of its only content ("| 11 | affects you. |" was "| 11 | Invisibility affects you. |")' },
  { key: 'rank-label-no-spell', raw: true,
    re: /(?:\*\*)?\b(?:cantrips?|\d(?:st|nd|rd|th))(?::)?(?:\*\*)?[ \t]*(?:[,;](?![ \t]*\S)|[,;][ \t]*[,;]|$)/m,
    why: 'a spell-list rank label followed by nothing or by a bare separator — the spell name was removed ("6th: ; 7th:" was "6th: Disintegrate; 7th: …")' },
  { key: 'unnamed-granted-feat',
    re: /\b(?:gain|take|select)s?\s+(?:the|either)\s+(?:skill\s+)?feat\b/i,
    why: 'a granted feat with no name — "You gain the skill feat" was "You gain the Multilingual skill feat"' },
  { key: 'unnamed-cast-or-effect',
    re: /\b(?:casts?|casting|the effects of(?: an?)?|such as|works like|affected by|destroyed by a)\s*(?=[.,;:)]|$)/im,
    why: 'a casting/effect clause whose spell name was removed ("you gain the effects of .", "The tattoo casts .")' }

  Expected recall on the current shipped data: the 5 shapes together flag every one of the 219 records in F5-F10; `unnamed-granted-feat` alone is exact for the 16 backgrounds + 4 feats.
  ⚠ Follow the file's own discipline (the removed "double space" shape, :86-89): each shape above is anchored on a grammatical or structural impossibility — an empty bullet, an empty cell, a verb phrase ending in punctuation — never on whitespace. Spot-check `unnamed-cast-or-effect` before landing it: "with a save." style clauses can trip a looser variant.

(B) `--dropped-uuid-labels` mode. Shape-matching is a proxy; the ground truth is the source. Add a mode that re-reads .import-src/pf2e/packs/pf2e, applies the SAME uuidLabel predicate the importer uses, and reports every (category, id, word) it would blank. This is exact — no false positives, no false negatives — costs one pack walk, and is the regression test for F1: after the narrowed predicate lands, this mode must report ZERO rows. Wire it into test/authoring-guards.test.ts alongside the SHAPES ratchet so the heuristic cannot loosen again.
---- VERIFY REASON:
Four independent failures. (1) Adding the 5 shapes takes scan()'s damaged set from 811 to 1143, blowing the BASELINE=890 ratchet at test/authoring-guards.test.ts:239/241 — the change breaks the very test it is to be wired into, and F12 never mentions BASELINE. (2) empty-table-cell needs two adjacent pipes and MISSES its own documented example "| 11 | affects you. |" (30 hits, 1 target). (3) rank-label-no-spell misses the real text it cites — bloodline-imperial "6th: , 7th: , 8th: Quandary", bloodline-elemental "1st: ; 3rd:", whispering-staff "- **4th** , Detect Scrying" all MISS; it only fires when the empty label ends the line. (4) The recall claim is false: 142 of a conservative 198-record damaged set go unflagged, including verified-damaged gamtu-hat, way-of-the-vanguard, bloodline-imperial, razmiri-mask-porcelain, whispering-staff.
---- CORRECTED FIX:
Salvage, then re-spec. KEEP: empty-list-item (24 hits, 22 targets — good precision) and the `raw` opt-out plus the scan() edit, which is mechanically sound (replaces :79-80, not ":76-82"). DROP or rewrite empty-table-cell and rank-label-no-spell — neither matches its own why-string example. unnamed-cast-or-effect fires on 294 records with ~20 targets (>90% FP) and still misses gamtu-hat and razmiri-mask-porcelain. unnamed-granted-feat is NOT "exact for the 16 backgrounds + 4 feats": 26 hits including 9 plain false positives (feats/advanced-weaponry "chosen when you gain the feat:", feats/natural-ambition "you can select the feat later", feats/free-heart, heritages/ancient-elf "to gain the feat.", heritages/nomadic-halfling, feats/wormskin, feats/eidolons-wrath, feats/general-training, heritages/polyglot-android).
Any shape that survives must ship with a BASELINE raise in the same commit. Wrong citation: the "double space" caution is at scan-damaged-descriptions.mjs:57-61, not :86-89.
Part (B) is the sound half but its premise needs one fix: the mode must replay the importer's effects-drop (:248) and journals-drop (:251) BEFORE applying uuidLabel. Without that it permanently reports ancestries/kholo and ancestries/tripkee (refs Compendium.pf2e.journals.JournalEntry.45SK8rdbbxvEHfMn.JournalEntryPage.xBV8oGvecw7abZxc / .FhIJtKcINEe7fipX — genuine 16-char Foundry ids that the narrowed predicate still blanks), so "must report ZERO rows" could never go green.
